# Add 57 reviewed retro projects across home computers, consoles, PCs, operating systems, applications and development tools.

Add 57 reviewed retro projects across home computers, consoles, PCs, operating systems, applications and development tools.

Date: 2026-10-18 · Review: ready

Snapshot check: upstream heads were not checked; pinned evidence is a historical snapshot.

- bwdos-mads-optimized-atari-dos / Runtime profiles (no-evidence-found): Atari 8-bit DOS with XL/XE BASIC handling and optional RAM disks/clock devices. README memory-use numbers describe components, not a complete universal machine minimum.

- atari-romdos-bootcode-archeology / Runtime profiles (no-evidence-found): Experimental single-sector disk requires ROMDOS ROM present. Formatting/writing DOS through original ROM cannot reproduce modified single-sector loader, so start from provided template; README explicitly identifies this limitation. No independently tested RAM/model minimum.
- atari-romdos-bootcode-archeology / AI (no-evidence-found): Unknown: inspected bounded primary documentation, implementation and latest commit text contain no explicit AI-contribution attribution. Absence is not evidence of non-use.
- bbc-basic310-atari-xlxe-port / Runtime profiles (no-evidence-found): README explicitly requires XL/XE-compatible computer, at least 64KB RAM and floppy drive, with interpreter under OS ROM. Native NMOS 6502 is documented; later 65C02 BASIC versions are not this port. TIME ticks differ PAL/NTSC.
- bbc-basic310-atari-xlxe-port / AI (no-evidence-found): Unknown: inspected bounded primary documentation, implementation and latest commit text contain no explicit AI-contribution attribution. Absence is not evidence of non-use.

### Identity (reviewed)

Semantically commented and reassemblable disassembly unifying the Dragon 32 and both Dragon 64 BASIC ROM variants.
- https://github.com/prime6809/DragonRom/blob/fdc2e808357748ae12d392e6a93fa41bbad1bfef/README.md
- https://github.com/prime6809/DragonRom/blob/fdc2e808357748ae12d392e6a93fa41bbad1bfef/DragonROM.asm

### Classification (reviewed)

Actual BASIC dispatch/operator tables, memory variables and annotated routines establish substantial analysis, not a bare opcode dump. Author dates initial disassembly to 2006 and completed comments to 2020; credits Inside the Dragon and Color BASIC Unravelled. No general licence file in pinned tree; annotations and original commercial ROM code retain separate provenance.
- https://github.com/prime6809/DragonRom/blob/fdc2e808357748ae12d392e6a93fa41bbad1bfef/README.md
- https://github.com/prime6809/DragonRom/blob/fdc2e808357748ae12d392e6a93fa41bbad1bfef/DragonROM.asm

### Source CPU (reviewed)

Native inspected assembly establishes 6809 source architecture; no minimum runnable machine inferred.
- https://github.com/prime6809/DragonRom/blob/fdc2e808357748ae12d392e6a93fa41bbad1bfef/README.md
- https://github.com/prime6809/DragonRom/blob/fdc2e808357748ae12d392e6a93fa41bbad1bfef/DragonROM.asm

### Target CPU (reviewed)

Inspected native implementation or opcode-output tables establish 6809 target architecture; no full minimum runtime configuration inferred.
- https://github.com/prime6809/DragonRom/blob/fdc2e808357748ae12d392e6a93fa41bbad1bfef/README.md
- https://github.com/prime6809/DragonRom/blob/fdc2e808357748ae12d392e6a93fa41bbad1bfef/DragonROM.asm

### Build (reviewed)

Makefile invokes external lwasm with model defines and writes three ROM/listing pairs. check target compares against external ../ROMS reference dumps, absent from tree. Byte-identity remains unknown. No upstream software was compiled or executed. No candidate software was compiled or executed in this pass; build/run/play/byte-identity flags remain unknown.
- https://github.com/prime6809/DragonRom/blob/fdc2e808357748ae12d392e6a93fa41bbad1bfef/README.md
- https://github.com/prime6809/DragonRom/blob/fdc2e808357748ae12d392e6a93fa41bbad1bfef/DragonROM.asm

### Runtime profiles (no-evidence-found)

Targets Dragon 32, Dragon 64 ROM mode and Dragon 64 RAM-mode BASIC. No independent ROM installation, boot or exact memory minimum verified.
- https://github.com/prime6809/DragonRom/blob/fdc2e808357748ae12d392e6a93fa41bbad1bfef/README.md
- https://github.com/prime6809/DragonRom/blob/fdc2e808357748ae12d392e6a93fa41bbad1bfef/DragonROM.asm

### AI (no-evidence-found)

Unknown: inspected bounded primary documentation, implementation and latest commit text contain no explicit AI-contribution attribution. Absence is not evidence of non-use.

BeebAsm-format annotated Atom disk operating-system source, WD1770 adaptation and disk-management utilities.

### Classification (reviewed)

Inspected LOAD, filename/catalogue and floppy I/O routines have semantic annotation. WD1770 variant documents 2015 port, added formatting/verification and GDOS/SDDOS-derived commands; GDOS 1.5 source names Gerrit Hillebrand. Treat this repository as one coherent DOS/tool suite. No general licence file found. Historical Acorn DOS, GDOS and SDDOS-derived contributions are separately attributed; public source is not a general rights grant.

### Identity (reviewed)

Software SID waveform/envelope emulation for Atari 8-bit, adapting C64 tune replay routines to POKEY output.
- https://github.com/ivop/atarisid/blob/d0bab7197b2d4d27cb4eb56478efc2c8a06aa9d5/README.md
- https://github.com/ivop/atarisid/blob/d0bab7197b2d4d27cb4eb56478efc2c8a06aa9d5/LICENSE

### Classification (reviewed)

Actual ADSR phases, shadow SID registers and interrupt-timed PCM generation establish substantial audio subsystem work. v6 uses per-channel sub-4-bit output and POKEY noise; v7 mixes three voices to 8-bit and has limited simultaneous noise reproduction. Original C64 replay routines are modified rather than emulating a complete C64. Root GPL-3.0 text and README expressly GPL version 3 only. Imported original tune routines/data retain their own author provenance; root licence not assumed to grant music rights.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native presentation viewer plus a host-side JSON-to-header compiler. PNG backgrounds, per-text placement/colours, typewriter animation, slide navigation and cartridge-RAM resume are implemented rather than only described.
- https://github.com/reini1305/gb_slideshow/blob/2cae81382c160d202415b90972d143c9a8ad2ebe/Makefile
- https://github.com/reini1305/gb_slideshow/blob/2cae81382c160d202415b90972d143c9a8ad2ebe/src/main.c

### Classification (reviewed)

Inspected generated-header implementation and bank switching in the viewer. Colour palettes are optional at runtime; included art is attributed separately. The source-level pipeline was inspected without running its generator. Root Apache-2.0 licence inspected. README separately attributes included RodrixAP pixel art under CC BY 2.0; code licence does not replace asset attribution.

### Target CPU (reviewed)

Inspected project-specific GBDK source/build target together with the pinned GBDK platform table: Game Boy/Color/Pocket/Mega Duck use SM83, SMS/Game Gear use Z80, and NES uses the mos6502 backend. This establishes the intended output architecture, not independent successful execution or a measured minimum.

### Build (reviewed)

GBDK Makefile invokes png2asset and src/generate_header.py on res/slides.json, then autobanks and links an MBC5 colour-aware ROM. Requires Python 3, external GBDK_HOME and source PNG assets. ### Identity (reviewed)

A compact but complete-looking native graphics experiment: the user enters a binary sequence with A/B, edits it, then the program repeats the pattern around an expanding square spiral using GBDK drawing primitives. The repository contains approximately 180 lines of project-specific C and pattern.gb.
- https://github.com/26F/pattern_maker_for_GBC/blob/cacadb2e326a1ae65a779b58bbaead214ad32e2d/src.c
- https://github.com/26F/pattern_maker_for_GBC/commit/cacadb2e326a1ae65a779b58bbaead214ad32e2d

### Classification (reviewed)

Source performs real input, plotting and spiral traversal. Despite the repository name, inspected source uses monochrome drawing calls and supplies no distinct colour-only implementation or minimum requirements. Treat as a bounded experiment, not a general graphics editor. No root licence found in the pinned tree; selected implementation files do not establish a general redistribution grant. Public availability is not a licence.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native VGM file browser/player streams uncompressed register-command music from cartridge SD storage. Separate Game Boy wave/sound, Game Gear stereo PSG and Master System PSG/OPLL implementations are present; inspected implementations decode VGM command bytes directly rather than executing a guest CPU.
- https://github.com/untoxa/VGM_player/blob/d2c30b2cce92fdbeaf2dba5d71d92f8633c505ff/LICENSE

### Classification (reviewed)

README names GB Everdrive X3/X5/X7, EZ-Flash Junior and an older Game Gear Everdrive. Master System target is additionally supported by actual inspected build and OPLL/PSG source. Compressed VGM and unimplemented command/chip cases are not claimed supported. Root MIT licence inspected. Bundled Petit FatFs preserves ChaN's separate redistribution notice in src/pff.c.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native benchmark cartridge measures eighteen checksum/noncryptographic/light-cryptographic algorithms against a generated 1024-byte buffer. Inspected C implements dispatch, paginated/sortable results and frame-counter timing, plus concrete xxHash32 and classical hash implementations.
- https://github.com/dmang-dev/hash-bench-gb/blob/7fa3a9cab6e8993d1b9539fed697a826ee5db344/src/main.c
- https://github.com/dmang-dev/hash-bench-gb/blob/7fa3a9cab6e8993d1b9539fed697a826ee5db344/src/xxhash32.c

### Classification (reviewed)

README describes abandoning five heavier algorithms after banked-call failures. Included heavy source files are not evidence those algorithms run in the current GB build. Some layout/timing statements retain older claims; results, speed ratios and methodology accuracy remain author reports. MIT covers the assembled benchmark work. NOTICE separately describes the algorithm implementations as re-expressions of public reference materials and lists their provenance; that assertion was not independently audited.
- https://github.com/dmang-dev/hash-bench-gb/blob/7fa3a9cab6e8993d1b9539fed697a826ee5db344/src/main.c

### Identity (reviewed)

Native experimental drone generator evolves a 32-nibble shift-register waveform, packs it into channel-3 wave RAM in a timer interrupt, and uses waveform data for scanline distortion. Period controls, waveform reseeding and playback state are implemented.
- https://github.com/vec2pt/gb-shifty/blob/357f59ba183e9b276ff234b99c7472ef5c7939f8/Makefile
- https://github.com/vec2pt/gb-shifty/blob/357f59ba183e9b276ff234b99c7472ef5c7939f8/README.md

### Classification (reviewed)

Prior README/root-source deferral is resolved by inspection of shiftwave, play_isr, scanline_isr and input implementation. README explicitly cites Shitwave inspiration; exact equivalence, audio quality and timing remain untested. Visualisation and seed improvements remain TODOs. No root licence found in the pinned tree; selected implementation files do not establish a general redistribution grant. Public availability is not a licence.
- https://github.com/vec2pt/gb-shifty/blob/357f59ba183e9b276ff234b99c7472ef5c7939f8/Makefile
- https://github.com/vec2pt/gb-shifty/blob/357f59ba183e9b276ff234b99c7472ef5c7939f8/README.md

Concrete geometry packing and byte-slicing implementation, not merely a list of formats. HDM-to-FDI hardcodes 2HD 1,261,568-byte geometry; reverse conversion expects a 4096-byte header. HDI handling assumes 4096-byte header and 512-byte alignment. Other BKDSK sizes are explicitly unverified; FlashFloppy now supporting HDM makes part of suite reference-oriented. Linux mounting is an author suggestion, not demonstrated target support. No licence file or broad reuse grant found in inspected root or source.
- https://github.com/barbeque/pc98-disk-tools/blob/4ef63c328ba4f39e578f6f9d96e7066ac90039f8/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Martin Lafaix resource manager/decompiler suite preserving REXX resource-container editing and .RES-to-.RC reconstruction plus C/Pascal packed-resource extraction helpers for OS/2 binaries.
- https://github.com/OS2World/DEV-UTIL-RESMGR/blob/1f319dda0d088012670abfd8b42c9bcf553f1120/README.md
- https://github.com/OS2World/DEV-UTIL-RESMGR/blob/1f319dda0d088012670abfd8b42c9bcf553f1120/readme.TXT

### Classification (reviewed)

Inspected actual NE/LX table parsing, resource add/delete/extract handling and multiple helper-source revisions. Executable packed resources require external helper, so the script alone is not complete. Documentation excludes decompilation of dialog resources from .RES and directs users to toolkit DLGEDIT; keep suite as one record. No processor assigned merely from file format/platform. Later packed-resource helper revisions are by Veit Kannegieser (2000–2002), not solely Lafaix; source3/rdcpp.pas depends on external ExeHdr, Objects and VpUtils Pascal units. Its note explicitly says Windows NE output differs from what rdc.cmd expects; no Windows target claimed. Root and release notes call it freeware; scripts say all rights reserved. No general modification/redistribution grant established; freeware is not assumed to mean open source.
- https://github.com/OS2World/DEV-UTIL-RESMGR/blob/1f319dda0d088012670abfd8b42c9bcf553f1120/README.md
- https://github.com/OS2World/DEV-UTIL-RESMGR/blob/1f319dda0d088012670abfd8b42c9bcf553f1120/readme.TXT

No predecessor machine-code input processor assigned. Data-only tools do not acquire a CPU from the PC-98 platform name, and native implementations do not acquire a source CPU from the host language.

### Identity (reviewed)

Eric Auer native DOS replacement GRAPHICS utility implementing resident CGA/EGA/VGA/MCGA screen capture and PostScript, Epson ESC/P and HP PCL printer output.
- https://github.com/FDOS/graphics/blob/feb52003bf88817052e0662219005a03046c5b93/doc/graphics.txt
- https://github.com/FDOS/graphics/blob/feb52003bf88817052e0662219005a03046c5b93/source/graphics.asm

### Classification (reviewed)

Read actual resident INT5/printer I/O, pixel readers, dithering and PostScript raster generation. Limited to documented standard modes; no VESA/high-resolution/Hercules support inferred. One coherent suite, not three inflated records for compile-time printer variants. CGA/EGA pixel-reader source credits DOSEMU vgaemu.c lineage; retain that attribution. Documentation/source identify GNU GPL; bundled doc/copying is GPLv2 text. No explicit later-version grant verified for this suite.
- https://github.com/FDOS/graphics/blob/feb52003bf88817052e0662219005a03046c5b93/doc/graphics.txt
- https://github.com/FDOS/graphics/blob/feb52003bf88817052e0662219005a03046c5b93/source/graphics.asm

### Build (reviewed)

Documentation supplies NASM commands selecting POSTSCRIPT, EPSON or HPPCL to produce graph-ps.com, graphpin.com and graph-hp.com from bundled assembly modules. Running requires compatible graphics mode and matching printer protocol; not tested. Read-only source/build inspection only; no candidate build, script, test or executable was run. ### Identity (reviewed)

Historical BSD Unix source port for PIC32 microcontrollers, preserving kernel, libc, userland utilities, native compiler environment and BSD filesystem while supplying board support and cross-build/image tools.
- https://github.com/RetroBSD/retrobsd/blob/a95be955bb0241c25452adf7e0b581bf329ac881/README.md
- https://github.com/RetroBSD/retrobsd/blob/a95be955bb0241c25452adf7e0b581bf329ac881/LICENSE

### Classification (reviewed)

Original-source portability/preservation work, not binary-derived decompilation or a new Unix clean-room reconstruction. README memory and boot claims remain attributed, without assuming their correctness as independently tested minima. README says BSD 2-clause style, but root LICENSE includes a third non-endorsement condition. Historical BSD source headers carry additional retained notices; preserve this mismatch rather than collapsing it to a blanket SPDX assertion.
- https://github.com/RetroBSD/retrobsd/blob/a95be955bb0241c25452adf7e0b581bf329ac881/README.md
- https://github.com/RetroBSD/retrobsd/blob/a95be955bb0241c25452adf7e0b581bf329ac881/LICENSE

### Source CPU (not-applicable)

Original-source BSD port and continued preservation, not an inspected predecessor binary. No historical CPU is inferred from the BSD version; source CPU not applicable at the demonstrated source-port scope.
- https://github.com/RetroBSD/retrobsd/blob/a95be955bb0241c25452adf7e0b581bf329ac881/README.md
- https://github.com/RetroBSD/retrobsd/blob/a95be955bb0241c25452adf7e0b581bf329ac881/LICENSE

### Target CPU (reviewed)

Documented output/support targets: Microchip PIC32, Microchip Explorer 16, Olimex PIC32-RetroBSD. Processor scope: MIPS32r2.
- https://github.com/RetroBSD/retrobsd/blob/a95be955bb0241c25452adf7e0b581bf329ac881/README.md
- https://github.com/RetroBSD/retrobsd/blob/a95be955bb0241c25452adf7e0b581bf329ac881/LICENSE

### Build (reviewed)

Actual MIPS coprocessor startup and board Makefiles inspected; target.mk selects Clang mipsel, mips32r2, o32, software float and LLVM linker. README documents Clang/LLVM cross build, generated SD image and kernel HEX, and separate QEMU-PIC32 dependency. No upstream code, build, test, emulator, installer or utility was executed. No candidate software was compiled or executed in this pass; build/run/play/byte-identity flags remain unknown.
- https://github.com/RetroBSD/retrobsd/blob/a95be955bb0241c25452adf7e0b581bf329ac881/README.md

README describes 128 KB on-chip RAM and no MMU, plus SD-card swapping; target.mk establishes mips32r2 compiler output. These are documented port constraints, not an independently measured minimal runtime profile.

## Research event notes

Broad discovery followed platform catalogues, developer portfolios, source-preservation and tooling links, with programmatic URL screening before detailed inspection. This is a diverse bounded pass, not an exhaustive inventory of every platform. Quality and completeness caveats remain explicit; preserved source, native reconstruction, homebrew and modern tooling are distinguished. No candidate code was executed. All earlier catalogue records are preserved.
